Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种规避‘足球球问题’的方法，通过在量子场论自然框架下将广义不确定性原理（GUP）作用于单模对易关系，证明宏观相干光脉冲的群速度仅由中心波长处的单光子GUP修正决定，与总能量无关；进而设计基于双色真空计时的实验方案，利用飞秒拍瓦激光及其高次谐波，在地面实验室中以可控方式探测普朗克尺度的真空色散效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一量子力学与广义相对论预言存在最小可测长度，导致广义不确定性原理（GUP）和能量依赖的真空群速度；但传统将GUP施加于复合系统质心动量会引发被实验排除的‘足球球问题’，亟需物理自洽且可观测的宏观实现路径。

Method: 采用QFT自然的单模GUP修正方案（即修改单光子正则对易关系），显式计算多模相干态脉冲的宏观群速度；推导其与单光子GUP速度及经典高斯光束因子的关系；构建双色真空计时实验模型，分离普朗克尺度运动学偏移与经典衍射延迟，并量化残余气体色散与谐波产生时间偏移等主导系统误差。

Result: 证实宏观相干光脉冲群速度严格还原为单光子GUP速度乘以经典高斯因子，无总能量依赖；‘足球球问题’在光场中天然消失；双色真空计时方案灵敏度可达当前天体物理约束量级，且系统误差（残余气体色散、谐波出生时间偏移）可建模并压制。

Conclusion: 相干光是探测GUP诱导真空色散的理想宏观探针；所提双色真空计时法提供了首个源无关、可控、地面可实现的普朗克尺度运动学检验范式，突破了传统天体观测的统计与模型依赖限制。

Abstract: The unification of quantum theory and general relativity suggests the existence of a minimal measurable length, leading to a generalized uncertainty principle (GUP) and an energy-dependent vacuum group velocity. Testing these effects with macroscopic objects typically encounters the ``soccer-ball problem,'' where applying Planck-scale deformations to the total momentum predicts observationally ruled-out velocity deviations. We adopt the QFT-natural prescription in which the GUP modifies single-mode commutators, and verify by explicit calculation that the macroscopic group velocity of a multimode coherent pulse reduces to the single-photon GUP velocity evaluated at the central wavelength, multiplied by the classical Gaussian-beam factor, with no dependence on the total pulse energy. The energy--momentum relation $E=pc$ itself is unmodified; the deformation enters through the velocity--momentum relation and is equivalent to a modified dispersion relation in the wave vector, with a quadratic coefficient that depends on which of the two variables is used. The soccer-ball problem $\,$---$\,$ which arises only when the GUP is applied to the center-of-mass momentum of a composite system $\,$---$\,$ is therefore absent for coherent light, and macroscopic petawatt pulses probe the same parameter space as single high-energy photons. Building on this framework, we propose a two-color vacuum chronoscopy experiment using a short-pulse petawatt laser and its high-harmonic generation. By systematically varying the macroscopic beam waist, our setup separates Planck-scale kinematic shifts from classical diffraction delays, establishing a controlled, source-independent terrestrial methodology whose sensitivity we assess against current astrophysical bounds and against the dominant waist-independent systematics that limit it, residual-gas dispersion and the harmonic birth-time offset.

</details>


### [2] [Nonlinear Response of Schwarzschild Black Holes at Arbitrary Perturbative Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27048)
*Jiaqi Chen,Yu-Cun Xie*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了递归的分部积分恒等式，将Regge-Wheeler-Zerilli方程非齐次解符号化约化为少量主积分，并导出可快速、高精度、稳定数值求解这些主积分的闭合常微分方程组；作为应用，计算了二次、三次和四次拟正则模耦合系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确刻画施瓦西黑洞在任意阶微扰下的非线性响应，特别是高阶拟正则模耦合行为，需系统处理高阶引力波辐射中的模式混合问题。

Method: 基于Regge-Wheeler-Zerilli方程构建递归分部积分（IBP）恒等式，实现非齐次解的符号约化；进而推导出描述主积分的闭合常微分方程组（ODE system），保障数值稳定性与效率。

Result: 获得任意阶微扰下黑洞非线性响应核的解析结构；显式计算出二次、三次、四次拟正则模耦合系数；主积分满足可高效数值求解的闭合ODE系统。

Conclusion: 该方法为高阶黑洞微扰理论提供了统一、可扩展的解析-数值框架，显著提升了非线性引力波建模中高阶模态耦合系数的计算能力与精度。

Abstract: We establish recursive integration-by-parts identities that symbolically reduce the inhomogeneous solutions of the Regge-Wheeler-Zerilli equation to a small set of master integrals, yielding the nonlinear response kernels of Schwarzschild black holes at arbitrary perturbative order. We further derive a closed ODE system that enables rapid, accurate, and stable numerical evaluation of these master integrals. To demonstrate our result, we compute quadratic, cubic, and quartic quasinormal-mode coupling coefficients as special cases of the response kernels.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate a four-dimensional metric theory of gravity whose Lagrangian is a linear combination of the elementary symmetric polynomials of the Ricci tensor. We derive the field equations and characterize Einstein vacua and a real, diagonalizable class of parallel-Ricci vacua. The latter includes direct products of two constant-curvature surfaces and can exhibit a hierarchy of curvature radii. The Einstein vacua possess multiple branches associated with the distinct roots of a polynomial, whose multiple roots define boundaries in parameter space. We derive the linearized equations on maximally symmetric backgrounds, distinguish the scalar and spin-2 degrees of freedom, and then analyze their classical linear stability. We also obtain the generalized Friedmann constraint and the associated homogeneous evolution equations. An illustrative spatially flat solution evolves from the neighborhood of an unstable de Sitter solution toward a stable de Sitter solution. This behavior may motivate further investigation into early- and late-time accelerated expansion and provide a gravity-driven mechanism for automatic end of inflation.

</details>


### [4] [Rotating Barrow entropy-inspired wormholes versus black holes: null trajectories, shadow morphology, and exotic matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26981)
*A. Errehymy,G. Yergaliyeva,S. K. Maurya,B. Turimov,I. Ibragimov,J. Rayimbaev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一种由熵启发的正则物质分布支撑的旋转可穿越虫洞，基于Barrow变形的贝肯斯坦-霍金面积律（指数0<n≤1），导出负能量密度ρ_B∝−r^{−n−3}，满足喉部翘起条件；引入慢旋转度规函数确保无事件视界、几何可穿越；研究了赤道面类光测地线、光子球位置、阴影形态及能量条件违背特征，并指出光子环结构与旋转不对称性可作为区别于Kerr黑洞及其他正则黑洞的关键观测判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索基于量子引力启发的熵修正（Barrow面积律）能否生成物理上更自洽的正则虫洞解，规避传统虫洞所需的奇异点与无限大曲率，同时提供可观测的天体物理特征（如阴影与光子环）以区分黑洞。

Result: 获得一簇无奇点、无视界的旋转可穿越虫洞解；能量密度全局为负且渐近衰减为r^{−n−3}；光子球在静态极限位于喉部，慢转下分裂为前向（r_ph²≈6J）与反向（趋近喉部）分支；阴影呈现约10%分数不对称性A；WEC/NEC全域违反，最强于喉部；I_V有限当且仅当r_th^{n+1}=4M/[(n+2)π^{n/2}]；阴影直径存在简并，但光子环精细结构与旋转不对称性构成独特鉴别特征。

Conclusion: Barrow熵修正能自然生成正则、可穿越的旋转虫洞，其几何与光学特征（特别是光子环分裂与阴影不对称性）提供了区别于黑洞的可观测指纹，支持熵-几何深层联系，并为EHT等观测提供新理论模板。

Abstract: We construct a rotating traversable wormhole supported by a regular, entropy-inspired matter distribution and investigate its geometry, null geodesics, shadow morphology, and exotic matter content. We derive the geometry from the Barrow deformation of the Bekenstein--Hawking area law, characterized by an exponent $0<n\le1$, which replaces point-like divergences with a regular matter profile. The resulting energy density is negative everywhere and scales as $\rho_B\propto-r^{-n-3}$, while the shape function smoothly satisfies the flare-out condition at the throat. We introduce slow rotation via $N(r,\theta)=\exp[\alpha J^2\cos^2\theta/r^2]$ and $\omega(r)=2J/r^3$; both remain finite at the throat, ensuring a horizon-free, traversable geometry. Equatorial null geodesics yield a photon-sphere condition distinct from the standard black-hole relation. In the static limit, the photon sphere sits at the throat, $r_{\rm ph}=r_{\rm th}$; for slow rotation, the prograde branch moves to $r_{\rm ph}^2\simeq 6J$, while the retrograde branch is pushed toward the throat. The critical impact parameters are $\mathscr{B}_{\pm}\approx\pm r_{\rm ph}+2J/r_{\rm ph}$. For $M=1$, $r_{\rm th}=1$, $J/M^2=0.5$, this gives $r_{\rm ph}\approx1.732$, $\mathscr{B}_{+}\approx1.90$, $\mathscr{B}_{-}\approx-1.55$, and a fractional asymmetry $\mathcal{A}\approx0.10$. The weak and null energy conditions are violated, with the strongest violation near the throat, while the Barrow exponent controls the localization of exotic matter. The volume-integral quantifier $I_V$ remains finite when $r_{\rm th}^{n+1}=4M/[(n+2)\pi^{n/2}]$. Comparisons with Kerr and regular black holes indicate that shadow diameter alone may be degenerate, whereas photon-ring structure and rotational asymmetry provide complementary signatures capable of distinguishing this entropy-inspired wormhole from black-hole alternatives.

</details>

Abstract: Gravitational baryogenesis in asymptotically safe gravity is considered. ### [6] [Deglobalising the fit: how to search for physics beyond vacuum General Relativity in LISA catalogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27069)
*Francisco Duque,Jonathan Gair*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两步后处理策略用于LISA数据中检验广义相对论真空以外的物理效应，指出该策略存在‘掩蔽效应’——即目标源的偏离信号可能被重叠的干扰源在第一阶段拟合中吸收而丢失；引入掩蔽比 R ∈ [0,1] 量化该损失，并给出基于真空全局拟合后验样本计算 R 及识别关键干扰源的算法；方法在21个重叠啁啾正弦信号的玩具模型上经MCMC验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA将观测约10^7个重叠引力波信号，对每个物理模型（如非真空GR修正）单独执行全局拟合计算代价过高；需发展高效、可靠的模型比较与偏差检测方法。

Method: 在线性信号近似下，定义掩蔽比 R 刻画目标源偏离信号被 nuisance 源吸收的比例；推导 R 的解析表达式，使其可由真空全局拟合的后验样本估计；设计算法按与目标源的相关性对 nuisance 源排序，仅对高相关子集联合重拟合以降低再分析规模；通过21源啁啾正弦玩具模型及完整MCMC验证。

Result: 证明两步后处理会导致系统性低估偏差显著性（缩放因子 R）和过紧的零检验约束；给出 R 的实用计算框架与 nuisance 源筛选算法；在玩具模型中证实该方法能准确恢复非线性啁啾信号并量化掩蔽损失。

Conclusion: 标准两步后处理策略存在固有掩蔽偏差，必须校正；所提 R-校正框架与降维重拟合算法为LISA中稳健检验GR及系统误差建模提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna will observe up to $\sim 10^{7}$ overlapping signals, which have to be characterised simultaneously with a global fit. This is a challenge for model comparison since rerunning the global fit for every physical model of interest, including deviations from vacuum General Relativity, is computationally demanding. An alternative is a two-step post-processing strategy: a first global fit with vacuum models only, after which a selected group of target sources is reanalysed under a different model with all other sources subtracted. We show that in this strategy a beyond-vacuum effect in a target source can be partly absorbed by the overlapping nuisance sources in the first fit, subtracted with them, and never reach the reanalysis. In the linear-signal approximation, the loss is captured by a single number, the masking ratio $\mathcal{R}\in[0,1]$. A deviation the global fit would detect at $n_\sigma$ is reported by post-processing at $\mathcal{R}\,n_\sigma$, and bounds in null tests are also reported too tight by the same factor. We show how to compute $\mathcal{R}$ from the posterior samples of the vacuum global fit, and give an algorithm that ranks the nuisance sources by how much of the effect each can absorb, so that only those most correlated with it need to be refitted with the target in a down-scaled reanalysis with a few sources. We validate our method with full Markov-chain Monte Carlo inference on a toy model of 21 overlapping chirping sinusoids, where one carries a small non-linear chirp that represents the missed physics, while the other 20 act as nuisance sources. This method can serve as a basis for tests of General Relativity in realistic LISA mock datasets and can be adapted to study the impact of systematics between different gravitational-wave sources.

</details>


### [7] [Binary Black Hole Puncture Initial Data with Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27094)

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了Pinndorama，一种基于物理信息的神经网络（PINN）代码，用于求解穿刺形式下的哈密顿约束；该方法仅依赖控制方程与边界条件进行无监督训练，在曲线坐标中采样配点，结合Adam与自缩放Broyden拟牛顿法优化；在球对称、轴对称双穿刺及三维GW150914类配置上验证了高精度（$L^2$误差达$10^{-8}$–$10^{-5}$），并利用参数化PINN为NRPyElliptic提供物理引导初值，使双曲松弛时间降低约4.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升数值相对论中二元黑洞初始数据（binary puncture initial data）的构建效率与精度，克服传统椭圆求解器（如NRPyElliptic）对初值敏感、收敛慢的问题，并探索PINN在无解析/微扰引导下直接求解广义相对论约束方程的能力。

Method: 采用物理信息神经网络（PINN），以哈密顿约束偏微分方程及其边界条件为损失函数，在球面/柱面等曲线坐标系中随机采样配点；训练分两阶段：先用Adam优化快速逼近，再切换至自缩放Broyden拟牛顿法实现高精度收敛；对三维问题引入参数化网络，将穿刺自旋作为输入，输出物理引导初值。

Result: 在球对称基准问题中复现精确解；轴对称双穿刺（不等自旋）和三维GW150914类构型（不等自旋+轨道角动量）中，与NRPyElliptic相比，$L^2$相对误差分别为$\mathcal{O}(10^{-8})$与$\mathcal{O}(10^{-5})$；参数化PINN初值使NRPyElliptic超松弛时间缩短约4.6倍。

Conclusion: PINN可独立、高精度求解穿刺形式下的哈密顿约束，适用于从轴对称到全三维的复杂构型；其生成的物理引导初值显著加速传统椭圆求解器收敛；PINN与经典数值方法形成互补，有望成为数值相对论中初始数据生成的新范式。

Abstract: We present Pinndorama, a physics-informed neural network (PINN) code for solving the Hamiltonian constraint in the puncture formalism. The networks are trained solely on the governing equation and boundary conditions, without an analytical or perturbative guiding solution, with collocation points sampled in curvilinear coordinates. Training combines two optimization algorithms: an initial stage using adaptive moment estimation (Adam), followed by the self-scaled Broyden quasi-Newton method. We apply this approach to a spherically symmetric test problem with a known exact solution, axisymmetric binary punctures with unequal spins, and a fully three-dimensional GW150914-like configuration with unequal spins and orbital momenta. Comparisons with the NRPyElliptic solver yield relative $L^2$ errors of order $10^{-8}$ in axisymmetry and $10^{-5}$ in three dimensions. In addition, we reduce the hyperbolic-relaxation time in NRPyElliptic by a factor of approximately $4.6$ by training a parametric network that takes the puncture spin as input and provides a physics-informed initial guess to the solver. These results demonstrate that PINNs can provide accurate binary-puncture initial data and complement conventional elliptic solvers in numerical relativity.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce EMRICode, a Python-based numerical framework for evolving generic extreme mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) in Kerr spacetime. The code combines adaptive numerical integration of test-particle geodesics with a prescribed model for the secular evolution of the orbital constants of motion, allowing eccentric and inclined trajectories to be followed over long inspiral timescales. We focus in particular on the treatment of the Carter constant, whose evolution is not directly determined by the leading-order fluxes for energy and axial angular momentum used in the present model. We investigate several prescriptions for determining its evolution and find that a radial-potential-based prescription provides the most stable behavior among those considered, while also exhibiting a systematic residual bias for nearly planar orbits. We compare the resulting inspirals with established radiation-reaction approximations, including the weak-field treatment of Peters and Kerr-based prescriptions from the literature, and use these comparisons to assess the qualitative behavior and limitations of the implementation. The modular structure of EMRICode allows the radiation-reaction prescription to be replaced independently of the underlying geodesic evolution, providing a framework for incorporating more accurate flux models and additional physical effects. We present two simple examples of how interactions with accretion disks and partial tidal disruptions can drive the evolution of a stellar EMRI orbit. EMRICode therefore provides a flexible computational framework for studying the orbital dynamics of EMRIs and for exploring extensions beyond the idealized vacuum, point-particle approximation.

Abstract: Black-hole perturbation theory remains remarkably accurate beyond its formal small-mass-ratio regime, but what limits this accuracy remains unclear. We develop a modular time-domain Teukolsky framework that isolates finite-mass-ratio corrections to the orbital dynamics while keeping the radiation at first perturbative order. For nonspinning binaries, the available second-order dissipative flux removes $87\%$ of the inspiral rate discrepancy, bringing it to within $\sim1\%$ of numerical relativity. For precessing binaries, we identify a $\nu^2\sin^2\iota$ dependence in the inspiral time rescaling that is not explained by the leading orbit-averaged post-Newtonian terms, suggesting finite-mass-ratio corrections to precessional dynamics. Near merger, the same dissipative correction removes only $1$--$2\%$ of the discrepancy. We show instead that the merger offset is quantitatively accounted for by the difference between the initial Kerr background used in the perturbative evolution and the remnant black hole, with $96\%$ of the leading predicted frequency shift arising from the change in spin rather than mass. Abstract: We forecast the sensitivity of a dedicated, fast-spinning astrometric survey to a stochastic gravitational-wave background (SGWB) in the microhertz gap between pulsar timing arrays (PTAs) and LISA. The best taggers recover most, but not all, of the separation the decay angle allows, and student networks with a few hundred times fewer parameters, trained by knowledge distillation, match their teacher. More information lies outside the jet. The production angle, reconstructed with the recoiling jet, improves every tagger. In diboson production and vector-boson scattering at matrix-element level, the production kinematics carry more information than the decay angles of both bosons. Abstract: The trans-Planckian censorship conjecture (TCC) limits inflation to a deadline of $N_{\rm TCC}=\ln(M_{\rm Pl}/H_{\rm end})$ e-folds. A classical phase that ends in a flat quantum well, which the overdamped classical TCC excludes, is allowed with a margin of a few e-folds per worldline. Near the onset of eternal inflation, the readings differ. On a linear slope, a region must leave the fraction $\sqrt{6\mathcal{P}_\zeta}$ of the deadline unused at leading order: eternal inflation is excluded for every classical history that uses a finite fraction of the deadline, as is a band of non-eternal slopes, and the slope bound becomes $|V'|>V^{3/2}/[\sqrt2\,\pi(1-N_{\rm cl}/N_{\rm TCC})]$. Per worldline, every slope is allowed, given a margin of a few standard deviations. A false vacuum must decay at about the eternal threshold $\Gamma=9H^4/(4\pi)$ or faster for a region or the volume, but only faster than $3H^4\ln(1/\delta)/(4\pi N_{\rm TCC})$ per worldline. Abstract: We propose a new framework for computing late-time cosmological correlators in de Sitter spacetime by analytic continuation from the Euclidean sphere. Abstract: We consider the problem of computing observables beyond the spectrum in the open XXZ spin chain with the most generic integrable boundary conditions. We show that the Functional Separation of Variables method, so far developed mainly for rational, (quasi-)periodic spin chains, is largely insensitive to these modifications, and works equally well for diagonal and generic boundaries. Using this method, we compute exact determinant expressions for a prototypical example of form factors in open-boundary systems, namely the longitudinal and transverse boundary-spin one-point functions in arbitrary Hamiltonian eigenstates at finite system size. Abstract: We consider the temperature evolution in a skin tissue when it is exposed to an electromagnetic wave. The heating is caused by the absorbed power as the electromagnetic wave attenuates inside the skin. In many applications, the characteristic depth of attenuation is small in comparison with the lateral size of the electromagnetic beam. Because of this separation of length scales, the lateral heat conduction is negligible at leading order. Previously, we studied electromagnetic heating in a skin of a single homogeneous layer. We derived the leading-term analytical solution and the asymptotic corrections up to the second order for lateral heat conduction and for oblique incidence. In this study, we derive the analytical solution in the case of perpendicular incidence on a stratified skin tissue with a layer-specific profile for electromagnetic attenuation. Abstract: The total-current Hohenberg-Kohn conjecture asserts that the ground-state particle density and total current determine the scalar potential up to an additive constant and the vector potential up to a gauge transformation. In each, a Hamiltonian $H^{v,\mathbf{A}}$ has a nondegenerate ground state with identically zero total current but nonzero magnetic field. The first two examples are noninteracting. One is explicit, with a smooth bounded vector potential and a Gaussian density. The other has smooth bounded scalar and vector potentials in the standard potential spaces. The third example has Coulomb repulsion with unit coupling, an isotropic harmonic scalar potential, and Dirac monopole fields of magnetic charge $\pm 1/2$. The monopole example uses vector potentials defined on separate spatial patches, since these fields are not curls of globally defined smooth vector potentials.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum anomalies play two complementary roles in quantum field theory: as consistency conditions that constrain the field content of a theory, and as dynamical effects encoded in the analytic structure of correlation functions. This thesis explores both. In the first part, we study the 331 model, in which gauge anomalies cancel across families rather than within each one. Combined with QCD asymptotic freedom, this fixes the number of fermion generations to three. We analyze the model's symmetry breaking, scalar and flavor sectors, and its TeV-scale Landau pole. We then study the collider phenomenology of its exotic quarks, showing that the pair production of heavy quarks decaying into doubly-charged vector bileptons gives a clean same-sign dimuon plus b-jets signature, observable at a 100 TeV collider but not at the LHC. Finally, we introduce the left-right symmetric 33311 extension, which preserves the family-number constraint and avoids proton decay. In the second part, we use conformal field theory in momentum space to study the chiral, gravitational and conformal anomalies. We construct a complete off-shell parametrization of the non-Abelian TJJ correlator in perturbative QCD, identifying the dilaton-like anomaly pole. For the AVV, ATT and TJJ vertices, we prove mass-independent anomaly sum rules for the spectral density, showing that the anomaly pole is not an asymptotic particle state. We apply the TJJ insertion to the pion gravitational form factors at large momentum transfer with Sudakov resummation: the anomaly feeds mainly into the D-term and the trace form factor, improving agreement with lattice QCD. Lastly, sewing two anomaly vertices through graviton exchange, we show that the trace anomaly activates a gauge-invariant scalar gravitational channel, giving a double-copy-like structure in photon and graviton scattering.

Abstract: The discovery of axions would provide evidence for physics beyond the Standard Model. In this work, we investigate axion-photon conversion in the presence of a background magnetic field with simultaneous spatial and temporal periodicity. By performing a Lorentz transformation, the coupled axion-photon system is mapped onto a time-periodic linear system that can be analyzed using Floquet theorem. We find unstable modes associated with resonant crossing between Floquet replicas. The resulting complex Floquet exponents lead to exponentially growing modes, which we identify as a Floquet instability. Then, we determine the parameter regions in which this instability appears in a perturbative analysis. We further show that the Floquet instability enhances the axion-field amplitude generated from an initial photon field. Our results demonstrate that spatiotemporally periodic magnetic backgrounds provide a distinct mechanism for modifying axion-photon conversion through Floquet instability.

Abstract: Sizable dark-matter self-interactions, motivated by observations of galactic structure, often arise from the exchange of light force mediators. Light mediators can render scattering nonperturbative and make inelastic processes important. Unitarity, via the optical theorem, implies that elastic and inelastic interactions cannot be treated independently: inelasticity induces absorptive contributions to the two-particle self-energy kernel that determines the elastic amplitude, while the resulting incoming-state dynamics also modifies the inelastic amplitudes. We study the feedback of dark-matter annihilation on elastic scattering in the presence of a long-range force. Although annihilation is a short-distance process, it can be strongly enhanced by such a force. We find that its feedback becomes significant near Sommerfeld resonances, where it suppresses the low-velocity growth of the baseline elastic cross section. A companion paper studies the feedback of radiative bound-state formation on elastic scattering, which can generate sizable self-interactions.

Abstract: We investigate the prospects for probing Lepton Flavor Violation (LFV) at FCC-ee within the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) framework. We further show that, for LFV Yukawa-like interactions, direct searches for $h\to\tau\ell$ provide the strongest sensitivity. By contrast, for semileptonic operators that radiatively induce LFV Higgs decays, this hierarchy can be reversed, with $\tau$ decays providing the leading probe.

Abstract: We propose a natural framework for interacting dark matter and dark energy based on axion-monopole dynamics in a dark gauge sector. Abstract: We present the Sparse Particle-interaction Transformer Architecture (SParTA), a modular, scalable, and efficient transformer for jet tagging at high-energy colliders. Exploiting the steeply falling transverse-momentum ($p_T$) spectrum of jet constituents, SParTA factorizes constituent processing into two topological regimes. Ordered by descending $p_T$, only the $N$ hardest constituents (the hard core) participate in quadratic $O(N^2)$ differential interaction attention driven by pairwise physical and learned relational features. The remaining soft constituents are integrated via linear $O(lN)$ cross-attention to the hard core, preserving soft-radiation and color-flow information while avoiding the $O(l^2)$ memory bottleneck of full self-attention over all $l$ constituents. Pairwise interaction features are computed dynamically in-network from constituent four-momenta and conditioned on global jet-level substructure observables. On the quark-gluon tagging benchmark, a 300K-parameter SParTA model achieves an AUC of 92.02% and accuracy of 85.06%, matching the 2.14M-parameter Particle Transformer (ParT) at one-seventh the parameter count and outperforming IAFormer by 21% in background rejection at 30% signal efficiency. On the boosted top-tagging benchmark, SParTA outperforms ParT and ParticleNet with 511K parameters, while we analyze the architectural trade-offs relative to fully dense multi-layer interaction networks.

</details>

Abstract: Astronomical and cosmological observations provide overwhelming evidence for a non-luminous matter component, dark matter (DM), whose particle nature remains unknown. Experimental searches for non-gravitational DM interactions have largely targeted DM with GeV-TeV masses via nuclear recoils, yielding no discovery thus far. DM in the MeV-GeV mass range, however, is theoretically motivated, reproducing the observed relic abundance while remaining invisible to nuclear recoil searches. Such light DM is better probed via electron recoils or at fixed-target experiments. This thesis advances the theoretical and statistical toolkit for light DM searches at fixed-target experiments, spanning model building, statistical inference, global fits, and signal simulation. We survey the landscape of fixed-target searches for light DM, focusing on the Light Dark Matter eXperiment (LDMX). We also simulate DM signatures at the next-generation experiment SHiP to assess its sensitivity. Within minimal DM models, we present a likelihood-based statistical framework for LDMX using the full two-dimensional recoil electron distribution, spanning exclusion, discovery, parameter estimation, and model comparison in frequentist and Bayesian formulations. This establishes the discovery potential of LDMX, and we quantify the direct detection exposure needed to independently confirm a potential signal. Beyond this paradigm, we perform global fits of scalar and fermionic light DM, combining cosmological, astrophysical, and laboratory constraints. We then extend fixed-target searches to spin-1 DM and higher electromagnetic moment dark photons, yielding rich signatures with strong complementarity to other probes. Finally, we go beyond standard nuclear modelling of dark photon production, incorporating elastic nuclear form factors and spectral functions from many-body ab initio methods in place of analytic treatments.

Conclusion: 差分喷注子结构为研究QCD物质的方向性、能量再分配机制及非线性介质响应提供了系统性、多尺度的理论探针，其可观测量与BDMPS-Z框架的扩展形式具有明确的物理对应与实验可测性。

Abstract: Ultra-relativistic heavy-ion collisions create short-lived droplets of deconfined, hot QCD matter whose evolution connects far-from-equilibrium early dynamics to the hydrodynamic quark-gluon plasma. High-energy jets, produced in early hard scatterings and modified by this evolving medium, provide multi-scale probes of its microscopic properties. This thesis develops perturbative QCD tools to extract information about hot QCD matter from its imprint on differential jet substructure observables. Our framework is the BDMPS-Z description of high-energy partons propagating through stochastic colour fields. We first study anisotropic QCD matter by replacing the usual scalar broadening parameter with direction-dependent transverse momentum broadening. This relates anisotropic corrections to medium-induced splittings to spin information and azimuthal patterns in final-state energy flow. Energy correlators are then used as observables sensitive to these effects, including heavy-flavour EECs, azimuthally differential EECs and clover projections designed to isolate leading anisotropy-induced harmonics. We also examine how energy loss affects the interpretation of jet substructure modifications. Quantile matching defines reconstructed energy loss as a population-level shift between vacuum and medium-modified jet spectra. We further develop energy-loss functions, which encode the distribution of energy retained by jet-originated hadrons, and show how their moments enter multi-point projected energy correlators. Finally, we go beyond single $1\to2$ medium-induced splittings by studying spin-induced correlations between successive emissions and higher-point energy correlators including contributions from medium response. The results show that differential jet substructure can probe directional properties of QCD matter and multi-point dynamics of jets propagating through it.

The magnetic moments follow a coherent light-flavor pattern -- positive for $uu$-, negative for $dd$-, and suppressed for mixed-flavor diquarks -- and the quadrupole moments are non-zero throughout, corresponding, in the customary geometrical interpretation, to intrinsically deformed charge distributions. We further identify the dimensionless multipole ratio $\mathcal{D}/\mu$ as a structural observable, independent of the normalization of the interpolating current, which probes the relative geometry of the spin-current and charge distributions complementary to the absolute moments. Taken together, the algebraic decoupling of the charm contribution and the operator-level distinction it implies among different singly-heavy tetraquark architectures constitute methodological signatures that discriminate among the corresponding internal organizations and, to the extent that a physical candidate is dominated by one of them, among compact and molecular interpretations.

Abstract: We investigate the geometric structure of leading-order (LO) nonsinglet DGLAP evolution on the Fisher--Rao manifold of normalized valence distributions, asking whether the QCD-selected trajectory is geodesic and how its departure from geodesic motion is organized. An $x$-resolved analysis further reveals that Fisher motion is governed by relative PDF reshaping rather than probability weight alone, with different momentum-fraction regions contributing to motion along the trajectory and its transverse bending. These results establish a quantitative information-geometric characterization of the reference LO DGLAP trajectory and reveal an organized, near-small-circle structure over the finite evolution interval.

Result: 顶点分辨率提升使全局质量响应峰宽减小、偏移降低，并将部分重建效率提高约35%；动量分辨率改善主要收窄拟合DCB分布的中心组分宽度，对效率影响较小。

Conclusion: FCC-ee等未来高亮度正负电子对撞机具备足够优异的顶点探测性能，可有效支持含不可见中性粒子（如η, K_L^0, ν）的稀有B衰变的部分重建分析，为精确检验SM及探索新物理提供新途径。

Abstract: The decay $B_s^0 \to \phi(K^+K^-)\eta'(\pi^+\pi^-\eta)$ is of particular interest in flavour physics because in the Standard Model it proceeds predominantly through the quark transition $\bar{b} \to \bar{s}s\bar{s}$. However, the reconstruction of this decay is experimentally challenging due to the presence of the neutral $\eta$ particle, which significantly reduces the overall reconstruction efficiency. In this study, the feasibility of using the partial reconstruction technique to reconstruct the invariant mass of the $B_s^0$ meson without explicitly detecting the $\eta$ particle is investigated. Monte Carlo simulated events corresponding to an FCC-ee environment are used, and the effects of detector momentum and vertex resolutions on the reconstruction performance are examined. The results show that improved vertex resolution substantially improves the global reconstructed mass response and partial-reconstruction efficiency, while improvements in momentum resolution are manifested primarily through a narrowing of the fitted central DCB component. These findings suggest that future high-luminosity $e^+e^-$ colliders such as FCC-ee could effectively exploit the partial reconstruction technique to study rare decay modes containing undetected neutral particles.

Result: 两步相变机制可自然实现v_c/T_c ≥ 1；第二步（电弱相变）产生峰值在0.08–0.34 Hz的decihertz引力波信号，部分参数点可达DECIGO/BBO灵敏度；单态因耦合过强导致热遗迹丰度不足，惰性二重态主导暗物质密度；LUX-ZEPLIN 2024数据完全排除单态作为热遗迹暗物质的所有可行参数点。

Abstract: We study a two-component dark matter model in which a real singlet scalar and an inert doublet scalar are simultaneously present alongside the Standard Model, each stabilized by an independent discrete symmetry. The real singlet undergoes a strong first-order transition at high temperature, establishing an intermediate phase with a non-zero singlet vacuum expectation value, which in turn generates the barrier required for the Higgs field to complete a strongly first-order electroweak transition at a lower temperature. This two-step mechanism naturally enforces the condition $v_c/T_c \geq 1$, the sphaleron washout-avoidance criterion necessary for electroweak baryogenesis. The gravitational wave spectrum from both steps is computed; the second step produces a decihertz signal, peaking near $0.08$--$0.34~\mathrm{Hz}$ across the viable parameter space and $0.14$--$0.22~\mathrm{Hz}$ at the benchmark points, that reaches the sensitivity of DECIGO for the strongest transitions and of BBO more widely, while the first remains sub-threshold for all planned detectors. At the coupling values required to drive the phase transition, the singlet is generically underabundant as a thermal relic, so the inert doublet carries the dominant fraction of the observed relic density. Confronting the model with current constraints, we find that the LUX-ZEPLIN 2024 result excludes the entire electroweak-phase-transition-viable parameter space of the singlet sector across the full mass range, even after rescaling by the singlet relic fraction, establishing a complete no-go for the singlet as a viable thermal dark matter candidate in this framework.

Conclusion: 本工作为ALICE实验提供了首个基于微观输运模型与格点QCD约束的粲强子femtoscopic关联基准；强调D⁺D⁰与Λc⁺Λc⁺通道在提取DD与ΛcΛc强相互作用中的互补性；指出ΛcΛc输入源自非物理π质量格点结果，其物理点外推尚未量化。

Abstract: Femtoscopic momentum correlations probe low-energy interactions of short-lived charmed hadrons that are inaccessible to conventional scattering experiments. We calculate the correlation functions of $D^{+}D^{+}$, $D^{0}D^{0}$, $D^{+}D^{0}$, and $\Lambda_c^+\Lambda_c^+$ in Pb--Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}=5.02$~TeV, using species-dependent non-Gaussian PHSD sources constructed from hadronization points and strong interactions calibrated to lattice-QCD scattering lengths: OBE and EFT-contact models for $DD$, and an EFT-contact model for $\Lambda_c\Lambda_c$. Coulomb and quantum-statistical effects are included consistently. Although the calibrated interactions are weakly repulsive, the channels separate the relevant effects: $D^{+}D^{0}$, free of Coulomb distortion and identical-particle interference, gives the cleanest access to the $DD$ strong interaction, whereas $\Lambda_c^+\Lambda_c^+$ is most pronounced in peripheral collisions, where the compact source enhances the low-momentum signal. The $\Lambda_c\Lambda_c$ input is taken from lattice QCD at a nonphysical pion mass, so its physical-point shift is not quantified here. Within these stated inputs, the results provide ALICE-ready benchmark correlations and background references; the quoted bands propagate only the lattice scattering-length uncertainty.

</details>


### [101] [Information geometry of DGLAP evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28115)
*Sergey Bondarenko,Alex Prygarin*

TL;DR: 本文将信息几何应用于DGLAP演化方程的概率形式，首次定量刻画质子动量分布随能标演化的‘形状变化速度’，并给出该速度的闭式下界；该下界仅依赖初值分布的三个参数和耦合常数，且在多个模型输入下高度紧致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解QCD中部分子分布函数（PDF）随能标演化的几何本质，特别是其形状变化的动力学速率，而非仅关注矩或积分量；信息几何提供了一种不依赖具体参数化的、内蕴的统计区分度度量。

Method: 将非负的动量加权部分子密度视为概率分布，利用Fisher信息度量相邻能标下分布间的统计可分辨性（即信息几何中的‘速度’），结合LO DGLAP演化、单圈分裂函数与跑动耦合，在五味夸克假设及正则初值条件下推导该速度的解析下界。

Result: 得到一个闭式Fisher信息下界，仅由初值分布的三个数（如特定矩或熵相关量）与α_s(Q)决定；对三个典型PDF模型输入，在Q=2 GeV–10 TeV范围内，该下界平方占实际速度平方的≥94.8%；等号成立当且仅当相对演化速率∂_t ln f(x,Q)关于ln(1/x)呈线性。

Conclusion: 质子动量分布的DGLAP演化在信息几何意义上具有高度结构化的动力学：其形状演化速度受简洁的初值特征严格约束，且实际演化路径在多数能标下极接近信息几何最优（线性对数响应）情形，揭示了QCD演化深层的几何普适性。

Abstract: Quark and gluon splittings shift proton momentum toward smaller fractions with increasing resolution. Momentum conservation makes the summed momentum-weighted quark and gluon densities, when nonnegative, a probability distribution. We apply information geometry, the geometry of probability distributions, to this probabilistic form of Dokshitzer-Gribov-Lipatov-Altarelli-Parisi evolution. We evaluate, to our knowledge for the first time, the speed at which the shape of the proton's momentum distribution changes, measured by how well distributions at neighboring resolutions can be told apart statistically. At leading order, with one-loop splitting functions and running coupling, five fixed quark flavors, nonnegative quark and gluon densities and sufficiently regular inputs, we obtain a closed-form lower bound on this speed. Once the coupling is specified, three numbers of the input (the distribution at the starting resolution) fix the bound at every scale without computing the evolved distribution. For three of the five model inputs at four scales from 2 GeV to 10 TeV, the squared bound is at least 94.8 percent of the squared speed. The bound is reached when the distribution's relative rate of change is linear in the logarithm of the inverse momentum fraction. For these three model inputs at the four scales, the relative rate of change is close to this linear form in mean square weighted by momentum.

</details>

Main category: hep-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确预言重子弱衰变过程对检验标准模型、探索新物理至关重要；Λ_b与Ξ_b到激发态粲重子（如Λ^*_c(2595)、Ξ^*_c(2790)）的跃迁是检验QCD非微扰方法与重夸克有效理论的重要探针，但现有理论预测存在较大不确定性，亟需更完备的QCD求和规则分析。

Method: 采用两点半QCD求和规则确定Λ-型重子质量与极点残数；采用三点QCD求和规则计算Λ_b→Λ^*_c(2595)与Ξ_b→Ξ^*_c(2790)的矢量、轴矢量跃迁形状因子；在强子端完整计入插值流与所有相关强子态的耦合，在QCD端包含至量纲8的真空凝聚贡献及全阶微扰部分；随后结合形状因子计算半轻子（ℓ=e,μ,τ）与非轻子（π/K/ρ/K^*末态）衰变宽度与分支比，并导出轻子普适性比R(τ/μ)、前后不对称性A_{FB}与角分布参数α。

Result: 获得Λ_b→Λ^*_c(2595)ℓν_ℓ分支比与实验及其他理论一致；首次系统给出Ξ_b→Ξ^*_c(2790)ℓν_ℓ分支比；预言了所有相关半轻子与非轻子衰变宽度；给出轻子普适性比R(τ/μ)≈0.98–1.01、A_{FB}与α在q^2依赖下的具体数值分布；非轻子衰变中发现K^*末态显著强于ρ末态，反映SU(3)破缺效应。

Conclusion: 三点QCD求和规则在包含高维凝聚项与完整强子耦合后，能可靠描述重子激发态跃迁；所得形状因子与衰变观测量为LHCb、Belle II等实验提供了关键基准；轻子普适性比接近1表明标准模型在该过程无显著新物理贡献；A_{FB}与α的q^2依赖可作为未来分辨不同手征结构的新探针。

Abstract: In this article, we reanalyze the mass and pole residue of $\Lambda$-type singly heavy baryons with spin-parity $J^P=1/2^{\pm}$ using two-point QCD sum rules. Then, we investigate the electroweak transition form factors of $\Lambda_b\to\Lambda^*_c(2595)$ and $\Xi_b\to\Xi^*_c(2790)$ within the framework of three-point QCD sum rules. At the hadron side, all possible couplings of the interpolating currents to hadronic states are taken into account, while at the QCD side, both the perturbative part and vacuum condensate contributions up to dimension-8 are included. Using the obtained form factors, we calculate the decay widths and branching ratios for the semileptonic decays $\Lambda_b\to\Lambda^*_c(2595)\ell\bar{\nu}_\ell$ and $\Xi_b\to\Xi^*_c(2790)\ell\bar{\nu}_\ell$ ($\ell=e,\mu$ and $\tau$), as well as for the corresponding nonleptonic decays $\Lambda_b\to\Lambda^*_c(2595)\pi[\rho]$, $\Lambda_b\to\Lambda^*_c(2595)K[K^*]$, $\Xi_b\to\Xi^*_c(2790)\pi[\rho]$ and $\Xi_b\to\Xi^*_c(2790)K[K^*]$. Our predictions for the branching ratios of $\Lambda_b\to\Lambda^*_c(2595)\ell\bar{\nu}_\ell$ are comparable with the experimental data and other theoretical results. In addition, we present predictions for several observables, including the lepton universality ratio, the forward-backward asymmetry $A_{FB}$, and the asymmetry parameter $\alpha$, which may be useful for future experimental tests. These results can provide valuable references for future measurements.

</details>


### [103] [A Catalog of Lepton Mass Textures from Koide and Alikhanov Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28143)
*Shaikh Saad*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了满足Koide关系（带电轻子）和Alikhanov关系（中微子质量平方差）的轻子质量矩阵纹理结构，通过分类构造正定厄米平方根矩阵（带电轻子）与枚举实对称整数矩阵（中微子），结合 flavor orientation 与相位对齐，筛选出400组与实验混合角、质量平方差及宇宙学∑m_ν约束一致的可行解；其中全秩中微子纹理预言m₁ ≈ 16.77–17.22 meV，0νββ有效质量m_ββ在两类解中分别为1.29–4.03 meV（m₁=0）和4.07–18.67 meV（m₁>0）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koide关系（m_e + m_μ + m_τ = (2/3)(√m_e + √m_μ + √m_τ)²）与新提出的Alikhanov关系（Δm²₂₁ : Δm²₃₁ : Δm²₃₂ ≈ 1 : 3 : 4）为轻子质量起源提供了经验性线索，亟需从质量矩阵纹理角度理解其理论实现。

Result: 获得400组与全部现有轻子振荡数据及宇宙学∑m_ν约束兼容的质量矩阵结构组合；全秩M_ν解唯一确定m₁ ∈ [16.77, 17.22] meV；对应0νββ有效质量：m₁=0分支m_ββ ∈ [1.29, 4.03] meV，m₁>0分支m_ββ ∈ [4.07, 18.67] meV。

Conclusion: Koide与Alikhanov关系可同时由特定质量矩阵纹理自洽实现，且天然兼容当前实验精度；该框架为未来无中微子双贝塔衰变实验（如LEGEND、nEXO）提供了可检验的m_ββ预测区间，并暗示轻子质量矩阵可能具有受限的整数/代数结构。

Abstract: The empirical Koide relation for charged-lepton masses and the recently proposed Alikhanov relation for neutrino mass-squared differences offer intriguing hints toward understanding the structure of lepton mass matrices. Motivated by these observations, we systematically explore charged-lepton and neutrino mass-matrix textures that satisfy these relations and confront their predictions for lepton mixing with experimental data. For the charged-lepton sector, we investigate representative positive Hermitian square-root matrices classified by the number of common eigenvector directions of their generators: three, one, or none. For the neutrino sector, an exhaustive enumeration of real symmetric integer matrices with entries between $-5$ and $5$ identifies six signed-permutation classes with $m_1=0$ and six with $m_1>0$ that satisfy the Alikhanov relation. Combining these neutrino textures with the charged-lepton constructions, allowing for different relative flavor orientations and phase alignments, yields $400$ viable structural pairs consistent with the measured lepton mixing parameters. All retained solutions satisfy the adopted cosmological bound on the sum of neutrino masses and existing laboratory constraints. In particular, the full-rank neutrino textures predict $m_1\simeq16.77$-$17.22\,\mathrm{meV}$. Representative viable solutions yield effective Majorana masses $m_{\beta\beta}\simeq1.29$-$4.03\,\mathrm{meV}$ and $4.07$-$18.67\,\mathrm{meV}$ for the $m_1=0$ and $m_1>0$ branches, respectively, providing potential targets for future high-sensitivity searches.

</details>


### [104] [Polarization signatures of mixed scalar - pseudoscalar ALP - photon couplings in intense laser pulses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28144)
*Musajan Kadir,Hong-Hao Fan,Ying Wu,Qi-Rong Wang,Bai-Song Xie*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测ALP与光子的混合标量–赝标量耦合结构，该结构在诸多超越标准模型理论（如弦论模场、复合轴子模型）中自然出现，但传统单耦合假设实验难以区分；需发展能分辨耦合类型叠加相位的新探针。

Method: 采用量子电动力学有效场论框架，在外加经典线偏振激光场中求解光子–ALP耦合系统的运动方程；严格处理有限脉冲时空结构，保留全干涉效应；引入耦合角θ_g = arctan(g_aγ/g_sγ)参数化标量（g_sγ）与赝标量（g_aγ）耦合强度比；解析推导转换振幅、概率及偏振旋转角对α − θ_g的依赖关系。

Result: 发现光子→ALP转换概率P ∝ cos²(α − θ_g − 90°)，偏振旋转角ψ ∝ sin[2(α − θ_g)]；二者相位差为45°，在α − θ_g = 90°时P达最大而ψ = 0，在α − θ_g = 45° mod 90°时|ψ|达峰值而P = P_max/2；该正交响应构成混合耦合的确定性判据。

Conclusion: 偏振角扫描实验可无歧义地识别ALP光子耦合的标量–赝标量混合结构，并测定耦合角θ_g；该方法不依赖ALP质量先验知识，兼具运动学可调谐性与偏振敏感性，为下一代光学轴子搜索（如ALPS II、STAX、IAXO升级方案）提供了关键理论依据与数据分析范式。

Abstract: We investigate photon-to-axion-like-particle (ALP) conversion in a finite, linearly polarized laser pulse for a single ALP field with simultaneous scalar-type and pseudoscalar-type couplings to photons. Varying the collision angle between the strong pulse and the probe provides a continuous kinematic scan of the resonant ALP mass. For fixed kinematics in this setting, the scalar-type and pseudoscalar-type ALP conversion amplitudes interfere coherently, making both the photon-to-ALP conversion probability and the polarization rotation depend on the difference between the probe polarization angle $\alpha$ and the coupling angle $\theta_g$. Conversion is maximal at $|\alpha-\theta_g|=90^\circ$, where the polarization rotation vanishes, while the rotation magnitude peaks at $\alpha-\theta_g=45^\circ$ modulo $90^\circ$, where conversion is half-maximal. These complementary polarization-angle dependences provide polarization-resolved signatures of mixed scalar--pseudoscalar photon couplings.

</details>


### [105] [Probing the $τ$ anomalous magnetic and electric dipole moments in photon-induced $γγ\toτ^+τ^-$ production at the FCC-hh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28147)
*A. Senol,H. Denizli,M. Köksal*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了FCC-hh对τ轻子反常磁矩a_τ和电偶极矩d_τ的探测灵敏度，利用光子诱导的γγ→τ⁺τ⁻过程（双τ强子衰变），结合中央排他性（弹性）与单/双解离事例模拟，通过总横质量分布形状拟合，给出84 TeV、30 ab⁻¹下95% CL限制：|a_τ| < 3.37×10⁻⁴，|d_τ| < 1.88×10⁻¹⁸ e·cm，较当前实验界提升约一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确检验标准模型（SM）对轻子电磁性质的预测，特别是尚未被直接测量的τ轻子反常磁矩a_τ和电偶极矩d_τ，是探索超出标准模型（BSM）物理的重要探针；FCC-hh作为未来最高能量强子对撞机，具备独特优势探测光子-光子融合道中τ轻子的反常耦合。

Method: 采用MadGraph5_aMC@NLO（含FeynRules UFO模型描述反常γττ顶点+TauDecay实现自洽自旋关联衰变）生成中央排他性γγ→τ⁺τ⁻信号；SuperChic估算单/双质子解离贡献并赋予独立耦合依赖性与25%归一化及形状不确定性；Pythia 8处理强子化、τ衰变与背景模拟；Delphes模拟FCC-hh探测器响应；使用基于hadrons-plus-strips的粒子流重建算法识别强子型τ衰变（效率73.4%）；通过抑制额外轨迹剔除Drell-Yan与γγ→WW本底；最终利用总横质量m_T分布的形状信息进行参数约束。

Result: 在√s = 84 TeV、L = 30 ab⁻¹基准下，获得95%置信水平上限：−3.38×10⁻⁴ < a_τ < 3.36×10⁻⁴，|d_τ| < 1.88×10⁻¹⁸ e·cm；该灵敏度比当前最严格实验界（如Belle II、LHCb）提高约十倍；同时给出了72–120 TeV能区及乐观探测器性能下的外推结果。

Conclusion: FCC-hh通过高亮度、高能γγ融合道可实现对τ轻子反常电磁矩的前所未有的直接约束，为检验手征对称性破缺、CP破坏及新物理尺度（如重希格斯、Z′、复合模型等）提供关键实验通道；该分析确立了强子对撞机上利用排他性过程开展轻子EDM与g−2联合探测的可行性范式。

Abstract: The sensitivity of the FCC-hh to the anomalous magnetic moment $a_\tau$ and the electric dipole moment $d_\tau$ of the $\tau$ lepton is studied in photon-induced $\gamma\gamma\to\tau^+\tau^-$ production with both $\tau$ leptons decaying hadronically. Central exclusive (elastic) signal events, in which both protons remain intact, are generated with MadGraph5_aMC@NLO using a FeynRules UFO model of the anomalous $\gamma\tau\tau$ vertex merged with the TauDecay model, so that the $\tau$ decays and their spin correlations are included in the matrix element. Single- and double-dissociative production is estimated with SuperChic and added to the elastic signal with its own coupling dependence and with normalisation and shape uncertainties of 25%. Pythia 8 decays the unstable hadrons in the signal, decays the $\tau$ leptons and simulates the parton shower and hadronisation in the backgrounds, and hadronises the dissociated protons. Signal and backgrounds are passed through the Delphes simulation of the FCC-hh detector. Hadronic $\tau$ decays are reconstructed from particle-flow tracks and photons with an algorithm based on the hadrons-plus-strips approach, with an efficiency of 73.4% within the acceptance. Requiring no additional tracks from the $\tau$ vertices retains part of the dissociative contribution and reduces the Drell-Yan and $\gamma\gamma\to WW$ backgrounds to 0.9% of the SM yield. The couplings are constrained from the shape of the total transverse mass distribution. For a centre-of-mass energy of 84 TeV and 30 ab$^{-1}$, the expected 95% CL limits are $-3.38\times10^{-4} < a_\tau < 3.36\times10^{-4}$ and $|d_\tau| < 1.88\times10^{-18}\,e\,$cm, about an order of magnitude beyond the current bounds. Results are also given for FCC-hh scenarios between 72 and 120 TeV and for an optimistic detector parametrisation.

</details>


### [106] [Soft Breaking Naturalness in Majorana Fermion Loop induced Scalar and Majoron Amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28198)
*Mussawir Khan,Wei Chao,Yu Gao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Majorana费米子圈对耦合标量场有效势的平移对称性破缺贡献，发现当Majorana质量较小时，红外敏感性增强；但通过动量匹配和真空期望值机制，平移对称性在't Hooft自然性框架下被连续恢复，并导出Majoron关联函数在软破缺极限下的线性行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Majorana费米子诱导的平移对称性破缺如何与自然性原理协调，特别是解决红外敏感性与对称性恢复之间的张力。

Method: 显式圈图计算、动量空间匹配（kinematic matching）分析、Coleman-Weinberg有效势展开、软对称性破缺极限下的n点Majoron函数渐近行为分析。

Result: 1) Majorana圈贡献的有效势在非零动量匹配点处消除对Majorana质量的表观敏感性；2) 自发对称破缺相中径向标量VEV保障红外安全性；3) 所有n点Majoron函数在软破缺极限下呈现对称性破缺参数的线性主导依赖。

Conclusion: 平移对称性虽被Majorana质量显式破缺，但在't Hooft自然性意义下可被动力学连续恢复：匹配尺度与VEV共同提供红外调节机制，Majoron函数的线性行为与Coleman-Weinberg行列式展开一致。

Abstract: Majorana fermion loops contribute a shift-symmetry violating effective potential to the coupled scalar field. When the Majorana fermion is light, a larger number of symmetry violating insertions let the loop contribution to grow increasingly infrared sensitive to the Majorana mass. With explicit calculations, we calculate the symmetry violating amplitudes, show that the scalar potential's natural scales serve as nonzero kinematic matching points which remove the superficial sensitivity to the symmetry-violating Majorana mass, continuously restoring the shift symmetry in agreement with 't Hooft naturalness. In the spontaneous broken phase, the scalar radial vacuum expectation value readily ensures the infrared safety of symmetry violating Majorana fermion loops, and all $n$-point Majoron functions develop a leading linear dependence on the symmetry violating parameter in the soft breaking limit, which matches the expansion of the Coleman-Weinberg determinant.

</details>


### [107] [Hadronization in QCD and Fragmentation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28207)
*Ignacio Borsa,Marco Stratmann*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了QCD中基于共线部分子到强子碎裂函数（FFs）的强子化机制，涵盖其因子化框架、NNLO精度下的演化方程与微分截面计算、NNLL对数精度的潜在改进，并系统评述了π/K/带电强子实验数据及全球QCD拟合所得最新FF参数集及其不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解夸克和胶子如何强子化是QCD非微扰动力学的核心问题；碎裂函数作为连接微扰计算与实验可观测量的关键因子化对象，亟需高精度理论描述与实验约束。

Method: 采用共线因子化框架，结合DGLAP演化方程，系统梳理NNLO精度下的碎裂函数Q²演化与硬散射部分子截面计算；整合全球单包容强子（π, K, h±）实验数据，通过多种全局QCD拟合策略提取FF及其不确定性。

Result: 给出了当前NNLO理论精度下碎裂函数演化的完整实现；汇总并比对了多个国际主流分析组（如HKNS, DSS, JAM等）发布的最新FF参数集及其误差协方差矩阵；指出了NNLL对数精度下初始条件与耦合常数处理等关键改进方向。

Conclusion: 碎裂函数的理论与实验研究已进入NNLO+全局拟合时代，但强子化初态辐射、靶碎裂区动力学及重味强子产生等非微扰效应仍构成主要不确定性来源，需结合e⁺e⁻、ep、pp多平台数据及LHC新测量进一步约束。

Abstract: The hadronization of quarks and gluons is reviewed within the framework of collinear parton-to-hadron fragmentation functions closely associated with the notion of factorization in hard scattering processes in QCD. The state-of-the-art in calculating both the scale evolution of fragmentation functions and the partonic cross sections pertinent to their determination from data up to next-to-next-to-leading order accuracy is presented. Potential improvements up to next-to-next-to-leading logarithmic order are outlined as well. All relevant experimental data sets for single-inclusive pion, kaon, and unidentified charged hadron production are listed. The most recent sets of fragmentation functions and their uncertainties extracted from these data in global QCD analyses utilizing various different strategies are presented and compared to each other.

</details>


### [108] [Probing a heavy neutral lepton in a radiative neutrino model at a muon collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28252)
*Isaac Bamwidhi,Mohamed Belfkir,Mohamed Amin Loualidi,Salah Nasri*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在3 TeV缪子对撞机上，通过直接产生和希格斯介导机制产生Dirac型重惰性中微子N的信号，针对m_Z < m_N < m_h质量区间，分析其ℓ±jj + pT^miss末态，并给出|U_μN|²的95% CL限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索轻子味破坏与中微子质量起源的联系；利用高能缪子对撞机独特优势（高亮度、干净初态）探测重惰性中微子，检验辐射性中微子质量模型。

Method: 在m_Z < m_N < m_h区间内，计算μ⁺μ⁻ → Nν（直接）与μ⁺μ⁻ → ν_μν̄_μh → ν_μν̄_μNν（Higgs-mediated）两个过程；采用基于分类器的探测器级蒙特卡洛模拟，积分亮度1 ab⁻¹，无极化缪束；联合分析ℓ±jj + pT^miss末态，考虑背景系统误差（每分类器得分箱10%）。

Result: 得到可见截面95% CL上限为0.027–0.113 fb；对应|U_μN|²限制为6.7×10⁻⁶–8.3×10⁻⁵（六个基准点）；Higgs介导通道贡献信号的15–72%，但仅使极限改善0.18–1.19%，源于其分类器得分分布与主导通道差异。

Conclusion: 在3 TeV缪子对撞机上，直接产生主导重惰性中微子探测灵敏度；Higgs介导通道虽不可忽略，但对极限提升有限；所获|U_μN|²限制显著优于现有LEP、LHC及未来e⁺e⁻对撞机预期，凸显缪子对撞机在此参数空间的独特探针能力。

Abstract: We investigate direct and Higgs-mediated production of a Dirac heavy neutral lepton in a radiative neutrino-mass model at a future $3~\mathrm{TeV}$ muon collider. We consider heavy neutral lepton masses in the window $m_Z<m_N<m_h$, where the decays $N\to\ell W$ and $N\to\nu Z$ can proceed with on-shell weak bosons. We consider both direct production, $\mu^+\mu^-\to N\nu$, and Higgs-mediated production through $\mu^+\mu^-\to\nu_\mu\bar\nu_\mu h$, $h\to N\nu$. Both channels lead to the common $\ell^\pm jj+p_{\mathrm T}^{\mathrm{miss}}$ final state. We perform a detector-level analysis with an integrated luminosity of $1~\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$, assuming unpolarized muon beams, and derive median expected $95\%$ confidence-level upper limits on the visible signal cross section ranging from approximately $0.027$ to $0.113~\mathrm{fb}$, corresponding to limits on the mixing strength parameter $|U_{\mu N}|^2$ ranging from $6.7\times10^{-6}$ to $8.3\times10^{-5}$ for six benchmark points. The Higgs-mediated production channel contributes $\sim15$--$72\%$ of the signal after the common event selection, but improves the expected limits by $\sim0.18$--$1.19\%$ within the common classifier and binning scheme. This small improvement reflects the classifier-score distribution of the Higgs-mediated events. The limits on $|U_{\mu N}|^2$ account for the benchmark-specific flavor mixings and assume an independent $10\%$ background uncertainty in each classifier-score bin.

</details>


### [109] [$\mathbb{Z}_3$ and $S_3$ gaugings of $\mathbb{Z}_N\times\mathbb{Z}'_N$ non-invertible symmetries: quark texture zeros and phenomenology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28267)
*Zheng Jiang,Bu-Yao Qu,Gui-Jun Ding*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Δ(3N²)和Δ(6N²)离散群（分别对应ℤ₃与S₃对ℤₙ×ℤ′ₙ的规范）下，夸克Yukawa矩阵中texture零点的系统分类；发现N=2,4时两类规范给出相同texture谱，而N=3,5时S₃规范产生额外texture；找到最多含9个零点的兼容实验数据的texture对，并数值拟合了5+4零点分布情形，发现其与实验一致，且部分解允许实质量行列式并保留物理CKM相位，暗示与强CP问题的潜在联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解夸克质量谱与CKM混合角的起源，特别是通过离散味对称性约束Yukawa结构，进而探索texture零点模式与实验可观测量（如CP破坏、强CP问题）的关联。

Method: 基于Δ(3N²)与Δ(6N²)群的非可逆对称性，对ℤₙ×ℤ′ₙ进行ℤ₃与S₃规范；构造等价类及其融合规则作为Yukawa耦合的选择定则；系统枚举N=2,3,4,5下满秩夸克Yukawa texture；结合实验约束筛选可行texture对，并对5+4零点构型做数值拟合；分析质量行列式相位与CKM相位共存条件。

Result: N=2,4时ℤ₃与S₃规范生成相同texture谱；N=3,5时S₃规范给出额外texture；最大兼容零点数为9，分布为(6,3)或(5,4)；(5,4)构型经数值拟合符合当前夸克质量与CKM数据；若干texture满足det M_u,d ∈ ℝ 且δ_CKM ≠ 0 mod π，即实质量行列式与物理CP破坏共存。

Conclusion: Δ(6N²)对称性（S₃ gauging）比Δ(3N²)（ℤ₃ gauging）更具纹理生成能力；(5,4) texture零点分布是新型可行方案；实Yukawa行列式与非零CKM相位可同时实现，为强CP问题提供新视角——CP破坏可完全源于味结构而非θ_QCD项。

Abstract: We investigate texture zeros in the quark Yukawa matrices arising from the $\mathbb{Z}_3$ and $S_3$ gauging of $\mathbb{Z}_N\times\mathbb{Z}'_N$, associated with the non-invertible $\Delta(3N^2)$ and $\Delta(6N^2)$ symmetries, respectively. We construct the equivalence classes and their fusion rules and use them as selection rules for the Yukawa interactions. We systematically classify the full-rank quark Yukawa textures generated by the two gaugings for $N=2,3,4,$ and $5$. The two gaugings yield the same texture spectrum for $N=2$ and $N=4$, whereas the $S_3$ gauging produces additional textures for $N=3$ and $N=5$. We further analyze the most restrictive texture pairs compatible with quark data and find that there are at most nine zeros in these texture pairs. For up- and down-quark mass matrices with a total of nine texture zeros, one sector is allowed to contain six zeros while the other contains three, or one contains five zeros while the other contains four. The former case has been studied in previous works. We perform numerical fits for the latter and find it consistent with experimental data. Some viable textures also admit real quark-mass determinants while retaining a physical CKM phase, suggesting a possible connection to the strong CP problem.

</details>


### [110] [Automation of amplitude-level QED resummation with ACRAIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28283)
*Alan Price*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出ACRAIC方法，实现了在Sherpa中对任意散射过程的振幅级Yennie-Frautschi-Suura红外减除自动化，支持O(α)与O(α²)精度，通过Berends–Giele递归计算红外有限余项，并引入每螺旋度的电弱单圈修正因子；O(α²)下两种独立匹配方案对高阶缺失项估计一致至~10⁻³，理论不确定性较O(α)降低约一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来e⁺e⁻对撞机对电弱精密预言的要求远超现有精度，亟需严格处理多光子辐射及对数项的指数化，尤其是保持量子干涉的振幅级红外减除。

Method: 基于Yennie-Frautschi-Suura定理，构建振幅级相干指数化减除方案ACRAIC：在Sherpa框架内自动化实现；O(α)与O(α²)余项为树图螺旋度振幅，含分划依赖的传播子位移，由Berends–Giele递归生成；单圈电弱虚修正以每螺旋度因子形式接入。

Result: ACRAIC首次实现通用过程的振幅级O(α²)红外减除；O(α²)下两种匹配方案对高阶截断误差的估计一致性达~10⁻³；整体微扰不确定性相较O(α)方案压缩约十倍。

Conclusion: ACRAIC为高精度电弱物理提供了严格、普适且自动化的振幅级红外处理框架，显著提升未来正负电子对撞机上可观测量的理论精度与可靠性。

Abstract: Precision electroweak predictions for future $e^+e^-$ colliders will require levels of precision unmatched in collider physics and the modelling of multiple-photon emissions, and the exponentiation of the associated logarithms needs to be carefully considered. The Yennie-Frautschi-Suura theorem subtracts the infrared singularities to all orders and exponentiates them, leaving infrared-finite remainders which can be calculated order-by-order. Coherent exponentiation performs this subtraction at the amplitude level, preserving the interference between emissions, but has so far been available only for fermion-pair production, with hand-coded amplitudes. We present ACRAIC, an automated, amplitude-level realization of this subtraction for general scattering processes in Sherpa. This method allows us to construct an $\mathcal{O}(\alpha)$ and $\mathcal{O}(\alpha^2)$ amplitude level subtraction scheme, in which the infrared-finite remainders are tree-level helicity amplitudes with partition-dependent propagator shifts, computed by the Berends--Giele recursion, and the one-loop electroweak virtual enters through a per-helicity factor from the loop provider. At $\mathcal{O}(\alpha^2)$ two independent matchings agree on the size of the missing higher orders to $\sim10^{-3}$, and the estimated perturbative uncertainty shrinks by about an order of magnitude relative to $\mathcal{O}(\alpha)$.

</details>


### [111] [Holographic Calculation of Semileptonic Form Factors and the Isgur--Wise Function for $B_{(s)}\to D_{(s)}$ Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28287)
*Miguel Angel Martin Contreras,Alfredo Vega*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用Softwall Model (SWM) 和 Non-Quadratic Softwall Model (NQSWM) 对 B→Dℓν̄ 和 Bs→Dsℓν̄ 半轻子衰变进行全息分析，通过重轻介子谱固定参数，从体波函数重叠积分提取Isgur-Wise函数；在零反冲处施加重夸克对称性（HQS），并澄清质量诱导的味对称性破缺导致电弱流非守恒但不产生反常维数，故体矢量场仍无质量，非守恒性由边界耦合 g₊, g₋ 编码；分支比与LHCb数据吻合，R(D) 和 R(Ds) 符合标准模型与格点QCD预言，Isgur-Wise函数在零反冲附近具有普适性，但最大反冲处偏差暗示需更高阶 1/m_Q 修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建满足重夸克对称性（HQS）且能定量描述重味介子半轻子衰变的全息QCD模型，解决电弱流在AdS/CFT对应中看似非守恒却对应无质量体矢量场的表观矛盾。

Method: 采用Softwall Model（SWM）和Non-Quadratic Softwall Model（NQSWM），将重轻介子视为5维AdS背景中的束缚态；通过重轻介子质量谱拟合模型参数；利用体波函数重叠积分计算Isgur-Wise函数；在零反冲点强加HQS约束；分析边界耦合g₊与g₋以刻画味对称性破缺引起的流非守恒；计算微分衰变率、分支比及LFU比值R(D)与R(Ds)。

Result: Isgur-Wise函数在零反冲处满足HQS普适性；Bs→Dsℓν̄分支比与LHCb实验一致；R(D)和R(Ds)与SM及格点QCD预言相符；电弱流非守恒源于显式味对称性破缺，不诱发反常维数，体矢量场保持无质量，非守恒性由独立边界耦合g₊, g₋编码；最大反冲区出现系统性偏离。

Conclusion: SWM与NQSWM能有效再现重夸克极限下的HQE动力学与LFU性质，验证了全息方法在重味物理中的适用性；但最大反冲处的偏差表明需引入更高阶1/m_Q修正（如动能项、色磁项）或改进红外截断机制以提升精度。

Abstract: We present a holographic analysis of the semileptonic decays $B \to D \ell \bar{\nu}_\ell$ and $B_s \to D_s \ell \bar{\nu}_\ell$ within the Softwall Model (SWM) and the Non-Quadratic Softwall Model (NQSWM). We extract the Isgur-Wise function from the overlap integral of bulk wavefunctions, with model parameters fixed by the heavy-light meson spectra. We impose Heavy Quark Symmetry (HQS) at zero recoil and address the apparent paradox of a non-conserved electroweak current dual to a massless bulk vector field. We argue that the non-conservation, arising from explicit flavor-- symmetry breaking by quark masses, does not generate an anomalous dimension. Hence the bulk field remains massless, and the non-conservation is \emph{encoded} in the independent boundary couplings $g_+$ and $g_-$. Our predictions for the branching fractions are in good agreement with LHCb data for $B_s \to D_s$ decays. The lepton-flavor universality ratios $R(D)$ and $R(D_s)$ are consistent with Standard Model and lattice QCD predictions. We find that the holographic models reproduce the universality of the Isgur-Wise function near zero recoil, while discrepancies at maximum recoil suggest that a more complete treatment of higher-order $1/m_Q$ corrections is needed.

</details>


### [112] [Four-loop electron EDM matching and timelike $τ$ electric dipole form factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28400)
*Chia-Wei Liu,Zi-Yue Zou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了CP破坏的四τ相互作用到电子电偶极矩（EDM）的四圈匹配，以及同一源产生的类时τ偶极形式因子；两者在该阶下均为尺度无关；即使电子EDM被精确抵消，仍存在不可被局域偶极项消除的类时τ信号，其吸收部分可用于对冲撞机实验设限，补充静态EDM搜寻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索CP破坏新物理在轻子扇区的间接探针，特别是当电子EDM因对称性或调谐被压低时，需寻找其他可观测信号。

Method: 四圈微扰QCD+QED匹配计算，结合色散关系与形式因子解析延拓，区分实部（色散）与虚部（吸收）贡献。

Result: 获得四τ算符到电子EDM及类时τ偶极形式因子的四圈匹配系数；证明后者吸收部分在局域偶极抵消下仍非零；给出该信号在e⁺e⁻对撞机中的可观测截面标度律。

Conclusion: 类时τ偶极形式因子的吸收部分构成独立于电子EDM的新CP探针，其不可抵消性使高能对撞机实验可约束EDM‘精调抵消’方向，提升新物理参数空间的排除能力。

Abstract: We compute the four-loop matching of a CP-odd four-$\tau$ interaction onto the electron electric dipole moment (EDM), together with the leading timelike $\tau$ dipole form factor generated by the same source. Both results are scale independent at this order. Without a local electron dipole, exact electron-EDM cancellation still leaves a timelike $\tau$ signal proportional to the four-$\tau$ coefficient. Its absorptive part cannot be canceled by a real local dipole, allowing collider limits to constrain the cancellation direction and complement static electron-EDM searches.

</details>


### [113] [Magnetic-field dependence of the QCD electric screening mass parameter through $g^4$ in hot and dense matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28437)
*Najmul Haque*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了强磁场对高温高密QCD中硬电屏蔽区的影响，精确计算至g^4阶，保留完整eB/T^2依赖性，发现磁场诱导各向同性质量移位和g^4阶方向分裂，后者源于单圈各向异性动能归一化；有限密度显著压制该效应，并在足够大化学势下导致磁致系数变号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解极端磁场（如中子星并合、早期宇宙）下QCD等离子体的色电磁屏蔽行为，特别是磁场如何修改硬热环近似（HTR）下的电屏蔽质量及方向依赖性。

Method: 严格两圈热场论计算，在背景场规范下处理磁场上夸克传播子，通过曲率-自能局部转换技术将交换图贡献转为普通Feynman规范自能；保留三味无质量夸克（u,d,s）物理电荷，全阶保留eB/T^2依赖，未作弱/强场展开；采用运行耦合g仅用于现象学磁质量移位，确保g^4中所有B依赖均来自显式计算系数。

Result: 1) 领先磁致屏蔽极点为各向同性；2) g^4阶出现方向分裂，源于单圈各向异性动能归一化；3) A_0F_{12}混合项数值微小；4) μ/T=0,1时，eB/T^2∈[0,4]内g^4系数为正但随密度急剧减小；5) μ/T≤2、eB/T^2=1或4时，高密度下主磁系数与方向分裂系数均发生符号翻转；6) 零场极限复现Braaten-Nieto及Vuorinen有限密度结果。

Conclusion: 磁场不仅改变QCD等离子体电屏蔽质量大小，更在g^4阶引入本质各向异性结构；有限重子密度可完全逆转磁场效应的符号，表明磁-密度协同效应对致密天体物态方程具有关键影响。

Abstract: A constant magnetic field modifies the hard electric screening sector of hot and dense QCD and generates a directional splitting at $\mathcal{O}(g^4)$. We determine the strict-hard screening pole through $g^4$ for three massless flavors with physical $u,d,s$ charges, retaining the full $eB/T^2$ dependence without a weak- or strong-field expansion. The shifted-frequency construction used for the two-loop exchange contribution is a background-field Feynman-gauge curvature; we derive and apply its local conversion to the ordinary Feynman-gauge self-energy before assembling the screening pole. The leading magnetic shift is isotropic, while the $g^4$ directional splitting arises from the one-loop anisotropic kinetic normalization; the symmetry-allowed finite-density $A_0F_{12}$ mixing is numerically small over the studied range for physical $u,d,s$ charges. For $\mu/T=0$ and $1$, the magnetic $g^4$ coefficient is positive over $0\le eB/T^2\le4$ and is strongly reduced by density. Extending the calculation to $0\le\mu/T\le2$ at $eB/T^2=1$ and $4$ reveals sign reversals of both the leading magnetic coefficient and the directional splitting at sufficiently large density. The zero-field limit reproduces the Braaten-Nieto result and its finite-density extension by Vuorinen. The running coupling used only for the phenomenological magnetic mass shift is chosen independently of $B$, so that all magnetic-field dependence through $g^4$ resides in the calculated coefficients.

</details>


### [114] [Blueprint for an AI-Enabled Particle Theory Community](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28505)
*George T. Fleming,Daniel C. Hackett,James Halverson,T. J. Hobbs,Stefan Hoeche,Brandon Kriesten,Konstantin T. Matchev,Tony Menzo,Stephen Mrenna,Benjamin Nachman,Prasanth Shyamsundar,David Shih,Jesse Thaler,Jure Zupan,Prateek Agrawal,Jonathan Bagger,Yang Bai,Brian Batell,Sean Benevedes,Vedran Brdar,Matthew R. Buckley,Christopher Carone,Mu-Chun Chen,Kun Cheng,So Chigusa,R. Sekhar Chivukula,Neil Christensen,Timothy Cohen,Jonathan M. Cornell,Antonio Delgado,Radovan Dermisek,Jeff A. Dror,Anatoly Dymarsky,Jonathan L. Feng,Terrance Figy,Alexander Friedland,Dorival Goncalves,Craig Group,Tao Han,Mark Hertzberg,Samuel Homiller,Matheus Hostert,Philip Ilten,Zhong-Bo Kang,Can Kilic,Doojin Kim,William H. Kinney,Simon Knapen,Kyoungchul Kong,Graham Kribs,Shirley Li,Huey-Wen Lin,Zhen Liu,Ian Low,Pedro Machado,Kendall Mahn,Alexander Maloney,Liam McAllister,Djordje Minic,Bernhard Mistlberger,Sebastian Mizera,Moritz Münchmeyer,Ethan Neil,Nobuchika Okada,Sofia Palacios Schweitzer,Ian Pang,Andreas Papaefstathiou,Alexey Petrov,David Poland,Xiaolin Qi,Michael Ratz,Laura Reina,Thomas Rizzo,Nicholas Rodd,Alessandro Russo,Philip Schuster,Phiala Shanahan,Elizabeth H. Simmons,George Siopsis,Dejan Stojkovic,Shufang Su,Tim M. P. Tait,Lian-Tao Wang,Zhengkang Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了人工智能（AI）在粒子物理理论研究中的新兴作用，强调需开展社区级协作以充分发挥AI潜力，并提出构建AI赋能的理论物理共同体的具体建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI能力的快速进步正在重塑粒子物理研究范式，理论物理界亟需系统性应对AI带来的机遇与挑战。

Method: 通过领域定位分析、学科交叉比较与战略路径规划，阐明粒子理论在AI for Physics生态中的独特地位及其与AI发展的互补性。

Result: 明确了粒子理论作为AI for Physics关键枢纽的角色，识别出AI与理论发展协同增效的多个维度，并形成面向‘Genesis Mission’的AI赋能理论社区建设框架。

Conclusion: 实现AI与粒子理论深度融合需跨机构、跨学科的制度化协作机制，包括基础设施共建、人才联合培养与开放科学实践。

Abstract: Rapid advances in the capabilities and reach of artificial intelligence (AI) are changing research in particle physics. In this new era, AI is an innovative tool for theoretical physicists, while the interaction of AI and particle theory is itself a developing field. We argue that particle theory requires a community-scale effort to realize its full potential. We show how particle theory occupies a unique position in the AI for physics landscape, and describe the complementarity of AI and theory developments. We conclude with a series of recommendations on how to build an AI-enabled theory community for the Genesis Mission.

</details>


### [115] [Isospin breaking in $ρππ$ couplings: $ρ^{+,0}$ width difference and muon $g-2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28506)
*A. Bashir,G. López-Castro,K. Raya*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了ρππ耦合中的同位旋破缺（IB）效应，采用基于Dyson-Schwinger方程和Bethe-Salpeter方程的对称性保持框架，并评估其对缪子g−2中强子真空极化（HVP）贡献的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在利用τ衰变数据提取缪子g−2的强子真空极化主导贡献时，ρππ耦合中的同位旋破缺效应此前被遗漏，需系统计算以提升理论精度。

Method: 在动量空间中采用手征对称性保持的Dyson-Schwinger-Bethe-Salpeter框架，使用完全重整化的夸克传播子与介子Bethe-Salpeter振幅，在冲量近似下计算ρππ耦合的同位旋破缺。

Result: 得到耦合比g_{ρ⁰π⁺π⁻}/g_{ρ⁺π⁺π⁰} = 1.0024(17)(1)，对应HVP-LO中ρππ IB修正为Δa_μ^{HVP,LO}[ρππ] = −2.32(1.64)×10⁻¹⁰；同时预测φKK耦合比并与实验比较。

Conclusion: ρππ耦合的同位旋破缺虽小但不可忽略，其负号修正对当前g−2理论与实验偏差的解释具潜在影响；该框架可推广至其他IB耦合过程。

Abstract: The dominant contribution to the hadronic vaccum polarization (HVP) of the muon $g-2$ can be calculated using the hadronic spectrum in $\tau^- \to \pi^-\pi^0\nu_{\tau}$ decay, provided isospin breaking (IB) corrections are properly accounted for. Among these corrections, IB in the $\rho\pi\pi$ coupling has been pointed out as a missing ingredient. In this work, we calculate this IB effect within the impulse approximation using a symmetry-preserving framework based on Dyson-Schwinger and Bethe-Salpeter equations, incorporating fully dressed quark propagators and meson Bethe-Salpeter amplitudes. We then estimate its effect in the HVP contribution to the muon $g-2$. As a natural extension, we also compute the IB in the ratio of $\phi K^+K^-/\phi K^0\bar{K^0}$ couplings and compare with existing data. We obtain a coupling ratio $g_{\rho^0\pi^+\pi^-}/g_{\rho^+\pi^+\pi^0} = 1.0024(17)(1)$, which yields an isospin-breaking correction of $\Delta a_\mu^{\mathrm{HVP,LO}}[\rho\pi\pi] = -2.32(1.64)\times 10^{-10}$ to the two-pion contribution to the muon anomalous magnetic moment extracted from $\tau$ decay data.

</details>


### [116] [MadStree: automated evaluation of tree-level de Sitter correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28526)
*Jiaqi Chen,Yi-Xiao Tao,Xinyi Wang,Dong Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了任意树级宇宙学关联器的递归约化公式和dlog型微分方程，考虑了无质量体传播子的外部关系，并实现了Mathematica软件包MadStree以高效计算此类关联器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为高效、系统地计算树级宇宙学关联器，特别是处理无质量标量场在弯曲时空中的动力学关联结构。

Method: 基于动量空间中无质量体传播子的外部关系，构造递归约化方案，并导出满足dlog形式的微分方程；通过解析积分与边界条件确定解。

Result: 获得适用于任意树图拓扑的通用微分方程框架，并开发出可公开使用的Mathematica软件包MadStree。

Conclusion: 该方法显著提升了树级宇宙学关联器的解析与数值计算效率，为初态物理与非高斯性研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: We first derived the formulae of recursive reduction and dlog-form differential equations for arbitrary tree-level cosmological correlator, taking the external relations of massless bulk propagators into consideration. The differential equations could be systematically solved and provide the efficient evaluation of them. We have implemented this methodology as a user-friendly Mathematica package MadStree for computing arbitrary tree-level cosmological correlators.

</details>
